  "figures": [
    {
      "caption": "Midpoint transport along a three-step label list. The left path of $i_{k}$ and the right path of $i_{k − 1}$ are generated with common midpoint $y_{k}$. Their full paths need not agree. After deleting $i_{0}$ on the left and $i_{3}$ on the right, the displayed midpoint multisets coincide. Unlisted paths are retained in both copies.",
      "figure_id": "fig:local-transport",
      "pdf_page": 31,
      "source_evidence": [
        {
          "anchor": "fig:local-transport",
          "exact_quote": "Midpoint transport along a three-step label list. The left path\nof $i_k$ and the right path of $i_{k-1}$ are generated with common\nmidpoint $y_k$. Their full paths need not agree. After deleting $i_0$\non the left and $i_3$ on the right, the",
          "source_object": "fig:0001"
        }
      ],
      "source_kind": "tikz_pgf",
      "tikz_pgf_source": "\\begin{tikzpicture}[x=1.65cm,y=1.3cm,>=Latex,\n particle/.style={circle,draw,minimum size=8mm,inner sep=1pt},\n made/.style={particle,fill=blue!12,draw=blue!65!black},\n tag/.style={particle,fill=gray!12,draw=gray!65!black}]\n\\node[anchor=east] at (-.55,1) {left copy};\n\\node[anchor=east] at (-.55,0) {right copy};\n\\foreach \\k in {0,...,3}{\n \\ifnum\\k=0\n   \\node[tag] (L\\k) at (\\k,1) {$i_\\k$};\n \\else\n   \\node[made] (L\\k) at (\\k,1) {$i_\\k$};\n \\fi\n \\ifnum\\k=3\n   \\node[tag] (R\\k) at (\\k,0) {$i_\\k$};\n \\else\n   \\node[made] (R\\k) at (\\k,0) {$i_\\k$};\n \\fi\n}\n\\foreach \\k/\\j in {1/0,2/1,3/2}{\n \\draw[blue!65!black,thick] (L\\k) -- node[fill=white,inner sep=1pt,font=\\small] {$y_\\k$} (R\\j);\n}\n\\draw[red!65!black,thick] (L0.south west)--(L0.north east);\n\\draw[red!65!black,thick] (R3.south west)--(R3.north east);\n\\node[font=\\small,anchor=south] at (0,1.4) {delete};\n\\node[font=\\small,anchor=north] at (3,-.4) {delete};\n\\end{tikzpicture}",
      "witness_description": "A diagram with two horizontal rows of circular nodes representing paths: top row labeled \"left copy\" containing nodes $i_0$, $i_1$, $i_2$, $i_3$, with $i_0$ crossed out with a diagonal slash and labeled \"delete\" above; bottom row labeled \"right copy\" containing nodes $i_0$, $i_1$, $i_2$, $i_3$, with $i_3$ crossed out with a diagonal slash and labeled \"delete\" below. Three upward-diagonal arrows connect nodes from the right copy to the left copy: from right $i_0$ to left $i_1$ labeled $y_1$, from right $i_1$ to left $i_2$ labeled $y_2$, and from right $i_2$ to left $i_3$ labeled $y_3$."
    },
    {
      "caption": "A schematic long comparison with $a_{0} = 2$ and $a_{1} = 4$. The left prefix and right suffix retain their original paths. Central pairs receive common proposed midpoints; an end pin copies the midpoint of its retained partner in the other copy. The arrows indicate the source of a pin, not equality of full paths. The changed left set is ${i_{2}, …, i_{6}}$, which excludes the common first tag. Deleting the crossed tags again leaves identical midpoint multisets.",
      "figure_id": "fig:silent-transport",
      "pdf_page": 39,
      "source_evidence": [
        {
          "anchor": "fig:silent-transport",
          "exact_quote": "A schematic long comparison with $a_0=2$ and $a_1=4$.\nThe left prefix and right suffix retain their original paths. Central\npairs receive common proposed midpoints; an end pin copies the midpoint\nof its retained partner in the other copy. T",
          "source_object": "fig:0002"
        }
      ],
      "source_kind": "tikz_pgf",
      "tikz_pgf_source": "\\begin{tikzpicture}[x=.92cm,y=1.45cm,>=Latex,\n particle/.style={circle,draw,minimum size=7mm,inner sep=1pt,font=\\small},\n made/.style={particle,fill=blue!12,draw=blue!65!black},\n pinned/.style={particle,fill=orange!18,draw=orange!70!black},\n old/.style={particle,fill=gray!15,draw=gray!65!black}]\n\\node[anchor=east] at (-.6,1) {left};\n\\node[anchor=east] at (-.6,0) {right};\n\\foreach \\k in {0,1}{\\node[old] (L\\k) at (\\k,1) {$i_\\k$};}\n\\foreach \\k in {2,3,4,5}{\\node[made] (L\\k) at (\\k,1) {$i_\\k$};}\n\\node[pinned] (L6) at (6,1) {$i_6$};\n\\node[pinned] (R0) at (0,0) {$i_0$};\n\\foreach \\k in {1,2,3,4}{\\node[made] (R\\k) at (\\k,0) {$i_\\k$};}\n\\foreach \\k in {5,6}{\\node[old] (R\\k) at (\\k,0) {$i_\\k$};}\n\\foreach \\k/\\j in {2/1,3/2,4/3,5/4}{\\draw[blue!65!black,thick] (L\\k)--(R\\j);}\n\\draw[orange!70!black,thick,->] (L1)--(R0);\n\\draw[orange!70!black,thick,->] (R5)--(L6);\n\\draw[red!65!black,thick] (L0.south west)--(L0.north east);\n\\draw[red!65!black,thick] (R6.south west)--(R6.north east);\n\\draw[decorate,decoration={brace,amplitude=4pt},gray!65!black]\n (-.35,1.45)--(1.35,1.45) node[midway,above=5pt,font=\\small] {silent prefix};\n\\draw[decorate,decoration={brace,amplitude=4pt},gray!65!black]\n (4.65,1.45)--(6.35,1.45) node[midway,above=5pt,font=\\small] {silent suffix};\n\\node[anchor=north,font=\\small,align=center] at (3,-.55)\n {gray: retained originals\\qquad blue: jointly generated pairs\\\\\n orange: paths pinned to a retained midpoint};\n\\end{tikzpicture}",
      "witness_description": "A diagram showing two horizontal rows of nodes labeled \"left\" (top) and \"right\" (bottom), each containing 7 circular nodes indexed $i_0, i_1, i_2, i_3, i_4, i_5, i_6$.\nAbove the left row, horizontal curly braces indicate \"silent prefix\" spanning $i_0, i_1$ and \"silent suffix\" spanning $i_5, i_6$.\nIn the left row: $i_0$ is gray with a diagonal slash; $i_1$ is gray; $i_2, i_3, i_4, i_5$ are blue; $i_6$ is orange.\nIn the right row: $i_0$ is orange; $i_1, i_2, i_3, i_4$ are blue; $i_5$ is gray; $i_6$ is gray with a diagonal slash.\nDiagonal lines connect the four central pairs: right $i_1$ to left $i_2$, right $i_2$ to left $i_3$, right $i_3$ to left $i_4$, and right $i_4$ to left $i_5$.\nArrows show pin assignments: from left $i_1$ down-left to right $i_0$, and from right $i_5$ up-right to left $i_6$.\nA legend below the diagram reads: \"gray: retained originals\", \"blue: jointly generated pairs\", and \"orange: paths pinned to a retained midpoint\"."
    }
  ],
